<!--
  ============================================================================
  Lumen AI - paid-ads landing page (single file, no external dependencies)
  ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  BEFORE LAUNCH - replace these placeholders:
    1. Contact Us : mailto:dreamonly@proton.me  (footer + legal pages)
    2. Legal links point to privacy.html and terms.html (standalone pages).
    3. Free-credit offer: 30,000 credits, granted automatically on install and
       never expiring. Confirm who provides those credits; if a backend / proxy
       is involved, revise the "no backend server" privacy wording accordingly.
    4. Verify both store URLs resolve (the slugs contain non-ASCII hyphens).
    5. For ad campaigns, pin the language with ?lang=en so the crawler and the
       ad text always see the same language.
  No analytics, no tracking pixels, no third-party scripts, no images.
  ============================================================================
-->
<style>
:root{
  --ink:#0f172a; --ink2:#334155; --muted:#64748b; --line:#e2e8f0;
  --bg:#f8fafc; --card:#ffffff;
  --brand:#4f46e5; --brand2:#6366f1; --brand-soft:#eef2ff; --brand-line:#c7d2fe;
  --cyan:#0ea5e9; --amber:#b45309; --amber-bg:#fffbeb; --amber-line:#fcd34d;
  --notice:#15803d; --notice-bg:#f0fdf4; --notice-line:#bbf7d0; --notice-solid:#16a34a; --notice-ink:#14532d;
  --ok:#059669; --dark:#0f172a;
  --r:18px; --shadow:0 12px 34px rgba(15,23,42,.07);
  --wrap:1180px;
}
*{box-sizing:border-box}
html{scroll-behavior:smooth;scroll-padding-top:88px}
body{
  margin:0;background:var(--bg);color:var(--ink);
  font-family:-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,"Segoe UI",Roboto,"Helvetica Neue","Noto Sans","Noto Sans CJK SC","Microsoft YaHei",Arial,sans-serif;
  line-height:1.68;-webkit-font-smoothing:antialiased;text-rendering:optimizeLegibility;
}
a{color:inherit;text-decoration:none}
button,select{font:inherit}
.wrap{max-width:var(--wrap);margin-inline:auto;padding-inline:24px}
.skip{position:absolute;inset-inline-start:0;top:0;transform:translateY(-200%);background:var(--brand);color:#fff;padding:10px 16px;border-radius:0 0 8px 0;z-index:99}
.skip:focus{transform:none}
:focus-visible{outline:3px solid var(--brand2);outline-offset:3px;border-radius:6px}

/* ---------- nav ---------- */
.nav{position:sticky;top:0;z-index:40;background:rgba(255,255,255,.88);backdrop-filter:blur(14px);border-bottom:1px solid var(--line)}
.nav-inner{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:28px;min-height:70px;flex-wrap:wrap}
.brand{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;font-weight:750;font-size:19px;letter-spacing:-.02em;white-space:nowrap}
.brand-mark{width:32px;height:32px;flex:0 0 auto;display:grid;place-items:center;border-radius:10px;color:#fff;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:800;letter-spacing:-.06em;line-height:1;background:linear-gradient(155deg,#a78bfa 0%,#7c4dff 48%,#4f3ce8 100%);box-shadow:0 5px 14px rgba(99,84,240,.32),inset 0 1px 1px rgba(255,255,255,.55);text-shadow:0 1px 1px rgba(60,30,140,.35)}
.brand-sub{font-size:11px;font-weight:600;color:var(--muted);letter-spacing:.06em;text-transform:uppercase;border-inline-start:1px solid var(--line);padding-inline-start:10px;margin-inline-start:2px}
.nav-links{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:26px;margin-inline-start:auto;flex-wrap:wrap}
.nav-links a{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:7px;font-size:14px;font-weight:600;color:var(--ink2);transition:color .18s}
.nav-links a:hover{color:var(--brand)}
/* nav icons: pure CSS shapes, currentColor follows hover, no images / SVG / emoji */
.nav-ico{position:relative;display:inline-block;width:14px;height:14px;flex:0 0 auto;color:var(--muted);transition:color .18s}
.nav-links a:hover .nav-ico{color:var(--brand)}
.ico-feat{background:linear-gradient(currentColor,currentColor) 0 0/5px 5px no-repeat,linear-gradient(currentColor,currentColor) 9px 0/5px 5px no-repeat,linear-gradient(currentColor,currentColor) 0 9px/5px 5px no-repeat,linear-gradient(currentColor,currentColor) 9px 9px/5px 5px no-repeat}
.ico-priv::before{content:"";position:absolute;left:2px;top:6px;width:10px;height:7px;border:1.5px solid currentColor;border-radius:2px;box-sizing:border-box}
.ico-priv::after{content:"";position:absolute;left:4px;top:1px;width:6px;height:6px;border:1.5px solid currentColor;border-bottom:0;border-radius:5px 5px 0 0;box-sizing:border-box}
.ico-faq{border:1.5px solid currentColor;border-radius:50%;box-sizing:border-box}
.ico-faq::before{content:"?";position:absolute;inset:0;display:grid;place-items:center;font-size:9px;font-weight:800;line-height:1}
.ico-contact{border:1.5px solid currentColor;border-radius:2px;box-sizing:border-box;overflow:hidden}
.ico-contact::before,.ico-contact::after{content:"";position:absolute;top:2px;width:1.5px;height:8px;background:currentColor;transform-origin:50% 0}
.ico-contact::before{left:2px;transform:rotate(-45deg)}
.ico-contact::after{right:2px;transform:rotate(45deg)}
.nav-actions{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px}
.langsw{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;border:1px solid var(--line);background:#fff;border-radius:999px;padding:3px 7px}
.langsw select{border:0;background:transparent;color:var(--ink2);font-size:12px;font-weight:700;padding:4px 4px;border-radius:999px;cursor:pointer;max-width:148px;outline:none;font-family:inherit}
.langsw select:focus-visible{box-shadow:0 0 0 2px var(--brand-line)}
.share{position:relative}
.share-btn{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:7px;border:1px solid var(--brand-line);background:var(--brand-soft);color:var(--brand);font-size:13px;font-weight:700;padding:8px 14px;border-radius:999px;cursor:pointer}
.share-btn:hover{background:#e0e7ff}
.share-ico{position:relative;display:inline-block;width:16px;height:16px;flex:0 0 auto}
.share-ico::before,.share-ico::after{content:"";position:absolute;left:3px;top:8px;width:11.2px;height:1.5px;background:currentColor;transform-origin:0 50%;border-radius:2px}
.share-ico::before{transform:rotate(-26.6deg)}
.share-ico::after{transform:rotate(26.6deg)}
.share-ico i{position:absolute;width:6px;height:6px;border:1.5px solid currentColor;border-radius:50%;background:var(--brand-soft);box-sizing:border-box}
.share-ico i:nth-child(1){left:0;top:5px}
.share-ico i:nth-child(2){right:0;top:0}
.share-ico i:nth-child(3){right:0;bottom:0}
.share-btn:hover .share-ico i{background:#e0e7ff}
.share-menu{position:absolute;inset-inline-end:0;top:calc(100% + 9px);min-width:190px;background:#fff;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:14px;box-shadow:var(--shadow);padding:7px;display:none}
.share-menu.open{display:block}
.share-menu a,.share-menu button{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:9px;width:100%;text-align:start;border:0;background:transparent;padding:9px 11px;border-radius:9px;font-size:13px;color:var(--ink2);cursor:pointer}
.share-menu a:hover,.share-menu button:hover{background:var(--bg);color:var(--brand)}
.share-menu .ico{width:18px;text-align:center;color:var(--brand);font-weight:700}

/* ---------- hero ---------- */
.hero{position:relative;overflow:hidden;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line);
  background:
    radial-gradient(900px 460px at 82% -8%,rgba(99,102,241,.13),transparent 65%),
    radial-gradient(680px 420px at 4% 8%,rgba(14,165,233,.10),transparent 62%),
    linear-gradient(180deg,#fff 0%,#f8fafc 100%);
}
.hero::before{content:"";position:absolute;inset:0;pointer-events:none;opacity:.5;
  background-image:radial-gradient(#cbd5e1 1px,transparent 1px);background-size:26px 26px;
  mask-image:linear-gradient(180deg,rgba(0,0,0,.35),transparent 70%);}
.hero-grid{position:relative;display:grid;grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1.02fr) minmax(0,.98fr);gap:56px;align-items:center;padding-block:74px 82px}
.hero-visual{order:1;display:flex;flex-direction:column;justify-content:center;gap:16px;min-width:0}
.hero-copy{order:2;min-width:0}
.visual-h1{font-size:clamp(26px,2.7vw,38px);line-height:1.24;letter-spacing:-.03em;margin:0;font-weight:800}
.visual-lede{font-size:14px;line-height:1.7;color:var(--ink2);margin:0;max-width:none}
.example-tag{align-self:flex-start;font-size:11px;font-weight:750;letter-spacing:.08em;color:var(--muted);background:#fff;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:999px;padding:4px 12px;margin:2px 0 -6px}
.eyebrow{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;font-size:12px;font-weight:750;letter-spacing:.08em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--brand);background:var(--brand-soft);border:1px solid var(--brand-line);padding:6px 13px;border-radius:999px}
h1{font-size:clamp(34px,4.2vw,55px);line-height:1.12;letter-spacing:-.035em;margin:20px 0 16px;font-weight:800}
h1 .grad{background:linear-gradient(100deg,var(--brand),var(--cyan));-webkit-background-clip:text;background-clip:text;color:transparent}
.lede{font-size:clamp(16px,1.55vw,18px);color:var(--ink2);max-width:38em;margin:0 0 20px}
.chips{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px;margin:0 0 26px}
.chip{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:7px;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:700;color:var(--ink2);background:#fff;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:999px;padding:6px 12px}
.chip::before{content:"";width:6px;height:6px;border-radius:50%;background:var(--ok)}
.chip.alt::before{background:var(--cyan)}

/* compliance disclosure - deliberately prominent, directly above the buttons */
.compliance{display:flex;gap:14px;background:var(--notice-bg);border:1px solid var(--notice-line);border-inline-start:5px solid var(--notice-solid);border-radius:14px;padding:18px 20px;margin:0 0 18px;box-shadow:0 6px 18px rgba(22,163,74,.09)}
.compliance-ico{flex:0 0 auto;width:30px;height:30px;border-radius:50%;background:var(--notice-solid);color:#fff;display:grid;place-items:center;font-size:17px;font-weight:800;font-style:normal;line-height:1}
.compliance strong{display:block;font-size:13px;letter-spacing:.05em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--notice);margin-bottom:4px}
.compliance p{margin:0;font-size:16px;line-height:1.55;font-weight:700;color:var(--notice-ink)}
.compliance .sub{margin-top:7px;font-size:13.5px;font-weight:500;color:var(--notice);line-height:1.55}

.cta-row{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:12px}
.btn{position:relative;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;min-width:238px;padding:13px 16px;border-radius:14px;border:1.5px solid var(--line);background:#fff;color:var(--ink);transition:transform .18s,box-shadow .18s,border-color .18s}
.btn:hover{transform:translateY(-2px);box-shadow:var(--shadow)}
.btn .bico{flex:0 0 auto;width:34px;height:34px;border-radius:10px;display:grid;place-items:center;font-size:19px;font-weight:700;background:var(--bg);color:var(--ink2)}
.btn .btxt{display:flex;flex-direction:column;line-height:1.3;text-align:start}
.btn .btxt b{font-size:14.5px;font-weight:750}
.btn .btxt small{font-size:11.5px;color:var(--muted);margin-top:2px}
.btn .barrow{margin-inline-start:auto;color:var(--muted);font-size:16px}
.btn.reco{border-color:var(--brand);background:linear-gradient(135deg,var(--brand),var(--brand2));color:#fff;box-shadow:0 10px 26px rgba(79,70,229,.28)}
.btn.reco .bico{background:rgba(255,255,255,.2);color:#fff}
.btn.reco .btxt small{color:rgba(255,255,255,.82)}
.btn.reco .barrow{color:#fff}
.btn-badges{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:10px;margin:11px 0 0}
.badge{font-size:11.5px;font-weight:700;padding:4px 10px;border-radius:999px;background:#f1f5f9;color:var(--muted);border:1px solid var(--line)}
.badge.hot{background:var(--brand-soft);color:var(--brand);border-color:var(--brand-line)}
.hero-tagline{font-size:15px;font-weight:700;color:var(--ink);margin:20px 0 2px}
.hero-sub{font-size:13.5px;color:var(--muted);margin:0}

/* pure-CSS sidebar mock: shows this is a sidebar extension, no images */
.mock{border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:20px;background:#fff;box-shadow:0 26px 60px rgba(15,23,42,.13);overflow:hidden;transform:rotate(-.5deg)}
.mock-bar{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;padding:11px 14px;background:#f1f5f9;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line)}
.dot{width:9px;height:9px;border-radius:50%;background:#cbd5e1}
.dot:nth-child(1){background:#f87171}.dot:nth-child(2){background:#fbbf24}.dot:nth-child(3){background:#34d399}
.mock-url{margin-inline-start:12px;flex:1;background:#fff;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:7px;font-size:10.5px;color:var(--muted);padding:5px 10px;white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis}
.mock-body{display:grid;grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1fr) 218px;min-height:330px}
.mock-page{padding:22px 20px;border-inline-end:1px solid var(--line)}
.line{height:8px;border-radius:5px;background:#e9eef5;margin-bottom:9px}
.line.w95{width:95%}.line.w80{width:80%}.line.w60{width:60%}.line.w88{width:88%}.line.w45{width:45%}
.mock-h{height:13px;width:66%;border-radius:5px;background:#cbd5e1;margin:2px 0 16px}
.mock-sel{margin:18px 0 10px;background:#e0e7ff;border-inline-start:3px solid var(--brand);border-radius:4px;padding:8px 10px;font-size:11px;color:#3730a3}
.mock-panel{background:#fafbff;display:flex;flex-direction:column;padding:14px 13px;gap:9px}
.panel-head{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:7px;font-size:12px;font-weight:750;padding-bottom:10px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line)}
.panel-logo{width:21px;height:21px;border-radius:7px;background:linear-gradient(155deg,#a78bfa,#5b3df5);color:#fff;display:grid;place-items:center;font-size:9px;font-weight:800;letter-spacing:-.06em;box-shadow:inset 0 1px 1px rgba(255,255,255,.45)}
.panel-tag{margin-inline-start:auto;font-size:9px;font-weight:700;color:var(--brand);background:var(--brand-soft);border-radius:999px;padding:2px 7px;text-transform:uppercase}
.bubble{font-size:10.5px;line-height:1.5;border-radius:10px;padding:9px 10px;max-width:92%}
.bubble.u{align-self:flex-end;background:var(--brand);color:#fff;border-bottom-right-radius:3px}
.bubble.a{align-self:flex-start;background:#fff;border:1px solid var(--line);color:var(--ink2);border-bottom-left-radius:3px}
.bubble.a i{display:block;height:6px;background:#e9eef5;border-radius:4px;margin:5px 0}
.bubble.a i:nth-child(2){width:78%}.bubble.a i:nth-child(3){width:56%}
.panel-chips{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:5px;margin-top:2px}
.panel-chips span{font-size:9px;font-weight:650;color:var(--brand);background:var(--brand-soft);border:1px solid var(--brand-line);padding:3px 7px;border-radius:999px}
.panel-input{margin-top:auto;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;gap:6px;background:#fff;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:9px;padding:8px 9px;font-size:10px;color:var(--muted)}
.panel-input span{color:var(--brand);font-weight:800}
.panel-foot{font-size:9.5px;color:var(--muted);text-align:center;letter-spacing:.01em}

/* ---------- sections ---------- */
.section{padding-block:82px}
.section.alt{background:#fff;border-block:1px solid var(--line)}
.shead{max-width:46em;margin-bottom:38px}
.shead h2{font-size:clamp(26px,3vw,38px);line-height:1.2;letter-spacing:-.03em;margin:14px 0 12px;font-weight:800}
.shead p{color:var(--ink2);font-size:16px;margin:0}
.grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(272px,1fr));gap:18px}
.card{background:var(--card);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:var(--r);padding:24px;transition:transform .2s,box-shadow .2s,border-color .2s}
.card:hover{transform:translateY(-3px);box-shadow:var(--shadow);border-color:var(--brand-line)}
.card-ico{width:44px;height:44px;border-radius:13px;display:grid;place-items:center;font-size:21px;font-weight:700;color:#fff;background:linear-gradient(135deg,var(--brand2),var(--cyan));margin-bottom:16px}
.card:nth-child(2n) .card-ico{background:linear-gradient(135deg,#0ea5e9,#22d3ee)}
.card:nth-child(3n) .card-ico{background:linear-gradient(135deg,#7c3aed,#a78bfa)}
.card h3{font-size:16.5px;margin:0 0 7px;font-weight:750;letter-spacing:-.01em}
.card p{font-size:13.5px;color:var(--muted);margin:0;line-height:1.62}
.sfoot{margin-top:26px;font-size:13.5px;color:var(--muted);border-inline-start:3px solid var(--brand-line);padding-inline-start:14px;max-width:60em}

/* ---------- free trial credits / model list ---------- */
.hero-promo{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;margin-top:13px;font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:var(--brand);border-bottom:1px dashed var(--brand-line);padding-bottom:2px}
.hero-promo:hover{color:var(--cyan)}
.credits{background:linear-gradient(180deg,#eef2ff 0%,#f8fafc 100%);border-block:1px solid var(--line)}
.credits .shead{margin-bottom:26px}
.mlabel{font-size:11px;font-weight:750;letter-spacing:.08em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--muted);margin:0 0 12px}
.model-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(272px,1fr));gap:12px;margin:0;padding:0;list-style:none}
.model{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:13px;min-width:0;background:#fff;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:14px;padding:14px 16px;box-shadow:0 4px 14px rgba(15,23,42,.04);transition:transform .18s,border-color .18s}
.model:hover{transform:translateY(-2px);border-color:var(--brand-line)}
.mlogo{flex:0 0 auto;width:38px;height:38px;border-radius:11px;display:grid;place-items:center;line-height:1}
.mlogo svg{width:32px;height:32px;display:block}
.mname{min-width:0;font-size:13.5px;font-weight:700;color:var(--ink);line-height:1.35;overflow-wrap:anywhere}
.model.more{background:transparent;border-style:dashed;box-shadow:none}
.model.more:hover{transform:none}
.mlogo.more{background:var(--brand-soft);color:var(--brand);border:1px solid var(--brand-line);font-size:20px}
.model.more .mname{color:var(--muted);font-weight:650}
.credits-note{margin-top:24px;display:flex;gap:12px;align-items:flex-start;background:#fff;border:1px solid var(--notice-line);border-inline-start:4px solid var(--notice-solid);border-radius:13px;padding:16px 18px;max-width:64em}
.credits-note .nmark{flex:0 0 auto;width:26px;height:26px;border-radius:8px;background:var(--notice-bg);color:var(--notice);display:grid;place-items:center;font-weight:800;font-size:14px;font-style:normal}
.credits-note p{margin:0;font-size:13px;color:var(--ink2);line-height:1.68}
.credits-note strong{display:block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.07em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--notice);margin-bottom:4px}
.credit-facts{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:center;gap:18px 34px;background:#fff;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:16px;padding:18px 24px;margin:0 0 26px;box-shadow:var(--shadow)}
.cf-amount{display:flex;align-items:baseline;gap:9px;flex:0 0 auto}
.cf-num{font-size:clamp(34px,4vw,46px);font-weight:800;letter-spacing:-.045em;line-height:1;background:linear-gradient(100deg,var(--brand),var(--cyan));-webkit-background-clip:text;background-clip:text;color:transparent}
.cf-unit{font-size:14px;font-weight:700;color:var(--ink2)}
.cf-list{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px 22px;list-style:none;margin:0;padding:0;flex:1 1 340px}
.cf-list li{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;font-size:13px;color:var(--ink2)}
.cf-ico{flex:0 0 auto;width:18px;height:18px;border-radius:50%;background:var(--notice-bg);color:var(--notice);font-size:11px;display:grid;place-items:center;font-style:normal;font-weight:800}
@media (max-width:640px){.credit-facts{padding:16px 18px;gap:14px}.cf-list{flex-basis:100%}}
.credit-source{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:baseline;gap:5px 10px;margin:-12px 0 24px;font-size:13px;color:var(--ink2)}.credit-source .cs-label{font-weight:650}.credit-source .cs-link{font-weight:700;color:var(--brand);border-bottom:1px solid var(--brand-line);word-break:break-all}.credit-source .cs-link:hover{color:var(--brand2)}.credit-source .cs-note{flex-basis:100%;font-size:12px;color:var(--muted)}@media (max-width:640px){.credit-source{margin:-4px 0 22px}}.fbottom .fop{color:#94a3b8}

/* ---------- privacy ---------- */
.privacy{background:var(--dark);color:#e2e8f0;position:relative;overflow:hidden}
.privacy::after{content:"";position:absolute;width:520px;height:520px;border-radius:50%;inset-inline-end:-160px;top:-220px;background:radial-gradient(circle,rgba(99,102,241,.22),transparent 68%);pointer-events:none}
.privacy .eyebrow{background:rgba(99,102,241,.16);border-color:rgba(129,140,248,.4);color:#c7d2fe}
.privacy .shead h2{color:#fff}
.privacy .shead p{color:#a9b6c8}
.pgrid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(238px,1fr));gap:16px}
.pcard{background:rgba(255,255,255,.045);border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.11);border-radius:15px;padding:20px}
.pcard .tick{display:inline-grid;place-items:center;width:24px;height:24px;border-radius:50%;background:rgba(52,211,153,.16);color:#6ee7b7;font-weight:800;font-size:13px;margin-bottom:11px}
.pcard h3{font-size:15px;color:#fff;margin:0 0 6px;font-weight:700}
.pcard p{font-size:13px;color:#9fb0c6;margin:0;line-height:1.62}
.flow{margin-top:34px;border:1px dashed rgba(255,255,255,.2);border-radius:16px;padding:22px}
.flow-label{font-size:11px;font-weight:750;letter-spacing:.09em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#94a3b8;margin:0 0 16px}
.flow-row{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;flex-wrap:wrap}
.flow-box{flex:1 1 175px;min-width:160px;background:rgba(255,255,255,.06);border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.14);border-radius:12px;padding:14px;text-align:center}
.flow-box b{display:block;color:#fff;font-size:13.5px}
.flow-box small{display:block;color:#94a3b8;font-size:11px;margin-top:3px}
.flow-arrow{color:#818cf8;font-size:22px;font-weight:700;flex:0 0 auto}
.flow-note{margin:16px 0 0;font-size:13px;color:#a5b4fc;text-align:center;font-weight:650}
.billing{margin-top:22px;display:flex;gap:12px;align-items:flex-start;background:rgba(52,211,153,.08);border:1px solid rgba(52,211,153,.25);border-radius:13px;padding:16px 18px}
.billing .mark{color:#6ee7b7;font-size:18px;font-weight:800;line-height:1.3}
.billing p{margin:0;font-size:13.5px;color:#c7d8cf;line-height:1.6}
.billing b{color:#fff}

/* ---------- faq ---------- */
details.faq-item{background:#fff;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:13px;margin-bottom:11px;overflow:hidden}
details.faq-item[open]{border-color:var(--brand-line);box-shadow:0 6px 18px rgba(79,70,229,.06)}
details.faq-item summary{list-style:none;cursor:pointer;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:14px;padding:17px 20px;font-weight:700;font-size:15px;color:var(--ink)}
details.faq-item summary::-webkit-details-marker{display:none}
details.faq-item summary::before{content:"?";flex:0 0 auto;width:24px;height:24px;border-radius:7px;background:var(--brand-soft);color:var(--brand);display:grid;place-items:center;font-size:13px;font-weight:800}
details.faq-item summary::after{content:"+";margin-inline-start:auto;color:var(--brand);font-size:21px;font-weight:600;line-height:1}
details.faq-item[open] summary::after{content:"−"}
details.faq-item .a{padding:0 20px 19px 58px;font-size:14px;color:var(--ink2);line-height:1.72}
details.faq-item .a p{margin:0}

/* ---------- policies (in-page placeholders, so links never 404) ---------- */
.policies{padding-block:56px;background:#fff;border-top:1px solid var(--line)}
.policies .shead{margin-bottom:22px}
.policies .shead h2{font-size:24px}
.pol-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(300px,1fr));gap:16px}
.pol{border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:14px;padding:20px;background:var(--bg)}
.pol h3{font-size:15px;margin:0 0 8px}
.pol p{font-size:13px;color:var(--muted);margin:0 0 8px;line-height:1.65}
.pol p:last-child{margin-bottom:0}
.pol-more{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;margin-top:4px;font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:var(--brand);border-bottom:1px solid var(--brand-line)}
.pol-more::after{content:"\2192";font-size:15px;line-height:1}
.pol-more:hover{color:var(--brand2);border-bottom-color:var(--brand2)}
[dir="rtl"] .pol-more::after{content:"\2190"}

/* ---------- footer ---------- */
footer{background:#0b1220;color:#94a3b8;padding-block:56px 24px}
.fgrid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1.5fr 1fr 1fr 1fr;gap:36px;padding-bottom:38px}
.fbrand .brand{color:#fff}
.fbrand p{font-size:13px;line-height:1.7;max-width:30em;margin:14px 0 0}
.fcol h4{font-size:12px;letter-spacing:.08em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#e2e8f0;margin:0 0 14px}
.fcol a,.fcol button{display:block;background:transparent;border:0;padding:0;margin-bottom:10px;font-size:13.5px;color:#94a3b8;cursor:pointer;text-align:start}
.fcol a:hover,.fcol button:hover{color:#fff}
.fbottom{border-top:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.1);padding-top:20px;display:flex;gap:14px;flex-wrap:wrap;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;font-size:12.5px}
.fbottom strong{color:#cbd5e1;font-weight:650}

/* ---------- responsive ---------- */
@media (max-width:980px){
  .hero-grid{grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1fr);gap:44px;padding-block:56px 62px;align-items:start}
  .hero-visual{order:2}
  .hero-copy{order:1}
  .mock{max-width:620px;margin-inline:auto;width:100%;transform:none}
  .hero-visual{max-width:620px;margin-inline:auto;width:100%}
  .nav-inner{gap:14px;padding-block:10px}
  .nav-links{order:3;width:100%;margin-inline-start:0;gap:18px;justify-content:center;border-top:1px solid var(--line);padding-top:10px}
  .nav-actions{margin-inline-start:auto}
  .fgrid{grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1fr) minmax(0,1fr);gap:28px}
}
@media (max-width:640px){
  .wrap{padding-inline:18px}
  .section{padding-block:58px}
  .hero-grid{padding-block:44px 52px}
  h1{font-size:33px}
  .brand-sub{display:none}
  .nav-links{gap:12px;justify-content:flex-start;overflow-x:auto;flex-wrap:nowrap;padding-bottom:4px}
  .nav-links a{gap:5px;font-size:13px;white-space:nowrap}
  .btn{min-width:100%}
  .compliance{padding:15px 14px;gap:10px}
  .compliance p{font-size:14.5px}
  .mock-body{grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1fr) 165px;min-height:290px}
  .mock-page{padding:16px 14px}
  .visual-h1{font-size:27px}
  .panel-head{font-size:11px;gap:5px;white-space:nowrap}
  .panel-tag{display:none}
  .flow-box{flex:1 1 100%}
  .flow-arrow{transform:rotate(90deg);margin-inline:auto}
  details.faq-item .a{padding-inline:20px;padding-inline-start:20px}
  .fgrid{grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1fr) minmax(0,1fr);gap:24px}
  .fbrand{grid-column:1 / -1}
  .fbottom{flex-direction:column;align-items:flex-start}
}
[dir="rtl"] .mock{transform:rotate(-.35deg)}
[dir="rtl"] .hero-visual{order:2}[dir="rtl"] .hero-copy{order:1}
[dir="rtl"] .flow-arrow,[dir="rtl"] .btn .barrow{transform:scaleX(-1)}
[dir="rtl"] .langsw select{direction:ltr}
@media (max-width:640px){[dir="rtl"] .flow-arrow{transform:rotate(90deg) scaleX(-1)}}
@media (prefers-reduced-motion:reduce){*{scroll-behavior:auto!important;transition:none!important}}
/* zh-CN H1 packs brand + feature keywords tightly: keep this slightly smaller
   size so phrases like "AI 聊天" never break across lines.
   Only affects lang="zh-CN"; English source and other locales are untouched. */
[lang="zh-CN"] .visual-h1{font-size:clamp(23px,2.25vw,31px);letter-spacing:-.02em}
@media (max-width:640px){[lang="zh-CN"] .visual-h1{font-size:23px}}

</style>
</head>
<body>
<a class="skip" href="#main" data-i18n="skip">Skip to content</a>

<header class="nav">
  <div class="wrap nav-inner">
    <a class="brand" href="#top"><span class="brand-mark" aria-hidden="true">AI</span>Lumen AI<span class="brand-sub" data-i18n="brand.sub">AI Sidebar Assistant</span></a>
    <nav class="nav-links" aria-label="Main navigation">
      <a href="#features"><i class="nav-ico ico-feat" aria-hidden="true"></i><span data-i18n="nav.features">Features</span></a>
      <a href="privacy.html" class="legal-link"><i class="nav-ico ico-priv" aria-hidden="true"></i><span data-i18n="nav.privacy">Privacy Policy</span></a>
      <a href="#faq"><i class="nav-ico ico-faq" aria-hidden="true"></i><span data-i18n="nav.faq">FAQ</span></a>
      <a href="#contact"><i class="nav-ico ico-contact" aria-hidden="true"></i><span data-i18n="nav.contact">Contact Us</span></a>
    </nav>
    <div class="nav-actions">
      <div class="langsw"><select id="langSel" aria-label="Language"></select></div>
      <div class="share" id="shareTop">
        <button type="button" class="share-btn" id="shareBtn" aria-haspopup="true" aria-expanded="false"><span class="share-ico" aria-hidden="true"><i></i><i></i><i></i></span><span data-i18n="nav.share">Share</span></button>
        <div class="share-menu" id="shareMenu">
          <button type="button" id="copyBtn"><span class="ico" aria-hidden="true">&#10697;</span><span data-i18n="share.copy">Copy link</span></button>
          <a href="#" id="shareEmail"><span class="ico" aria-hidden="true">&#9993;</span><span data-i18n="share.email">Share by email</span></a>
          <a href="#" id="shareX" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><span class="ico" aria-hidden="true">&#10023;</span>X / Twitter</a>
          <a href="#" id="shareLi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><span class="ico" aria-hidden="true">in</span>LinkedIn</a>
          <a href="#" id="shareFb" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><span class="ico" aria-hidden="true">f</span>Facebook</a>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
</header>

<main id="main">

  <!-- ============ HERO ============ -->
  <section class="hero" id="top">
    <div class="wrap hero-grid">
      <div class="hero-copy">
        <div class="chips" aria-label="Key points">
          <span class="chip" data-i18n="chip.free">Free AI Sidebar Assistant</span>
          <span class="chip alt" data-i18n="chip.noads">No-Ads</span>
          <span class="chip" data-i18n="chip.byok">Bring Your Own API Key</span>
          <span class="chip alt" data-i18n="chip.nosub">No Subscription Fee</span>
        </div>

        <!-- PRE-INSTALL DISCLOSURE: intentionally placed directly above the download buttons -->
        <div class="compliance" role="note" aria-label="Installation disclosure">
          <i class="compliance-ico" aria-hidden="true">i</i>
          <div>
            <strong data-i18n="compliance.label">Please read before installing</strong>
            <p data-i18n="compliance.text">This is a browser sidebar extension. Click button to go to official extension store for installation.</p>
            <p class="sub" data-i18n="compliance.sub">Lumen AI is a Chrome &amp; Edge sidebar extension. Installation takes place on the official extension store. No software or file is downloaded from this page.</p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div class="cta-row">
          <a class="btn" id="btnChrome" href="https://chromewebstore.google.com/detail/lumen‑ai‑%E2%80%94‑ai‑sidebar‑ass/cnndlmfbcioagnihcfkcldpalmfeijmh?utm_source=ext_sidebar" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">
            <span class="bico" aria-hidden="true">&#9673;</span>
            <span class="btxt"><b data-i18n="btn.chrome">Get it on Chrome Web Store</b><small data-i18n="btn.chromeSub">Chrome Web Store &#183; Free</small></span>
            <span class="barrow" aria-hidden="true">&#8599;</span>
          </a>
          <a class="btn" id="btnEdge" href="https://microsoftedge.microsoft.com/addons/detail/lumen‑ai‑%E2%80%94‑ai‑sidebar‑ass/eemiijmllplombajemahlclgiaoaipin" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">
            <span class="bico" aria-hidden="true">&#9678;</span>
            <span class="btxt"><b data-i18n="btn.edge">Get it for Microsoft Edge</b><small data-i18n="btn.edgeSub">Edge Add-ons Store &#183; Free</small></span>
            <span class="barrow" aria-hidden="true">&#8599;</span>
          </a>
        </div>
        <div class="btn-badges">
          <span class="badge" id="badgeChrome">Chrome Web Store</span>
          <span class="badge" id="badgeEdge">Edge Add-ons</span>
        </div>
        <p class="hero-tagline" data-i18n="hero.tagline">No sign-up. No subscription fee. Use your own AI API key.</p>
        <p class="hero-sub" data-i18n="hero.sub">Simple, powerful features. Accurate AI translation. A lightweight no-ads browser AI tool, popular among users who read, translate and take notes on the web.</p>
        <a class="hero-promo" href="#credits"><span data-i18n="hero.promo">Installing now? Get 30,000 free credits, valid for one month</span><span aria-hidden="true">&#8594;</span></a>
      </div>

      <!-- CSS-only illustration of the sidebar extension -->
      <div class="hero-visual">
        <h1 class="visual-h1"><span data-i18n="hero.title">Your AI Sidebar Assistant for Every Web Page</span></h1>
        <p class="visual-lede" data-i18n="hero.lede">Lumen AI is a free AI sidebar assistant for Chrome and Edge. Get web-page summarization, web translation for foreign trade, and free AI chat for browser reading &mdash; without leaving the page you are on.</p>
        <span class="example-tag" data-i18n="mock.example">Example</span>
        <div class="mock" aria-hidden="true">
        <div class="mock-bar"><span class="dot"></span><span class="dot"></span><span class="dot"></span><div class="mock-url">https://example.com/long-article</div></div>
        <div class="mock-body">
          <div class="mock-page">
            <div class="mock-h"></div>
            <div class="line w95"></div><div class="line w88"></div><div class="line w60"></div>
            <div class="line w95"></div><div class="line w80"></div>
            <div class="mock-sel" data-i18n="mock.sel">Selected text on the page</div>
            <div class="line w88"></div><div class="line w45"></div>
          </div>
          <div class="mock-panel">
            <div class="panel-head"><span class="panel-logo">AI</span>Lumen AI<span class="panel-tag" data-i18n="mock.tag">Sidebar</span></div>
            <div class="bubble u" data-i18n="mock.ask">Summarize this page</div>
            <div class="bubble a"><span data-i18n="mock.reply">Key points</span><i></i><i></i><i></i></div>
            <div class="panel-chips"><span data-i18n="mock.c1">Summarize</span><span data-i18n="mock.c2">Translate</span><span data-i18n="mock.c3">Ask</span></div>
            <div class="panel-input"><span data-i18n="mock.input">Ask anything about this page</span><span>&#8629;</span></div>
            <div class="panel-foot" data-i18n="mock.foot">Local API key &#183; No backend server</div>
          </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </section>

  <!-- ============ FEATURES ============ -->
  <section class="section" id="features">
    <div class="wrap">
      <div class="shead">
        <span class="eyebrow" data-i18n="feat.eyebrow">Features</span>
        <h2 data-i18n="feat.title">A focused toolkit, right in your sidebar</h2>
        <p data-i18n="feat.lede">An ai sidebar assistant for reading, writing and research. All features run in your browser sidebar on Chrome and Edge &mdash; no account, no ads, no subscription.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="grid">
        <article class="card"><div class="card-ico" aria-hidden="true">&#10022;</div><h3 data-i18n="feat.1t">Ask about selected text</h3><p data-i18n="feat.1d">Select any text on a web page and ask AI about it. Follow-up questions keep the multi-turn conversation context.</p></article>
        <article class="card"><div class="card-ico" aria-hidden="true">&#9672;</div><h3 data-i18n="feat.3t">Web page &amp; bilingual translation</h3><p data-i18n="feat.3d">Translate a selected phrase or the whole page. Side-by-side bilingual reading for foreign trade translation work.</p></article>
        <article class="card"><div class="card-ico" aria-hidden="true">&#9998;</div><h3 data-i18n="feat.4t">Quick local notes</h3><p data-i18n="feat.4d">Jot down notes while you browse. Notes are saved locally in your own browser, not on a server.</p></article>
        <article class="card"><div class="card-ico" aria-hidden="true">&#9638;</div><h3 data-i18n="feat.5t">Screenshot &amp; image text</h3><p data-i18n="feat.5d">Upload a screenshot to ask AI about the image, or extract text from it for reading and translation.</p></article>
        <article class="card"><div class="card-ico" aria-hidden="true">&#9656;</div><h3 data-i18n="feat.6t">Detect &amp; save page media</h3><p data-i18n="feat.6d">Detect audio and video resources on the current page and save them locally when you need them.</p></article>
        <article class="card"><div class="card-ico" aria-hidden="true">&#8984;</div><h3 data-i18n="feat.7t">Connect your AI provider</h3><p data-i18n="feat.7d">Works with major AI providers through your own API key. You choose the model and the provider.</p></article>
      </div>
      <p class="sfoot" data-i18n="feat.foot">Whether you need a chrome ai sidebar extension or an edge ai sidebar, Lumen AI combines a webpage summarizer, web page translation, free AI chat for browser and a foreign trade translation extension in one no-subscription ai extension.</p>
    </div>
  </section>

  <!-- ============ FREE TRIAL CREDITS ============
       "Logos" are CSS letter marks only - no image / svg / font assets.
       Model names are kept exactly as supplied; keep them identical to the
       extension store listing so the ad copy stays consistent. -->
  <section class="section credits" id="credits">
    <div class="wrap">
      <div class="shead">
        <span class="eyebrow" data-i18n="credits.eyebrow">Free credits with every install</span>
        <h2 data-i18n="credits.title">Install now and get 30,000 free credits</h2>
        <p data-i18n="credits.lede">Every new install is automatically granted 30,000 credits, valid for one month. Use them with the models below. The extension itself stays free, with no subscription and no ads.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="credit-facts">
        <div class="cf-amount"><span class="cf-num">30,000</span><span class="cf-unit" data-i18n="credits.unit">credits</span></div>
        <ul class="cf-list">
          <li><i class="cf-ico" aria-hidden="true">&#10003;</i><span data-i18n="credits.fact1">Granted automatically on install</span></li>
          <li><i class="cf-ico" aria-hidden="true">&#10003;</i><span data-i18n="credits.fact2">Valid for one month</span></li>
          <li><i class="cf-ico" aria-hidden="true">&#10003;</i><span data-i18n="credits.fact3">Use with the models below</span></li>
        </ul>
      </div>
      <div class="credit-source">
        <span class="cs-label" data-i18n="credits.provider">Credits provided by this separate platform:</span>
        <a class="cs-link" href="https://shop.1235566.xyz/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">shop.1235566.xyz</a>
        <span class="cs-note" data-i18n="credits.providerNote">That platform's own terms and privacy policy apply.</span>
      </div>
      <p class="mlabel" data-i18n="credits.listLabel">Models available with the free credits</p>
      <ul class="model-grid">
        <li class="model"><span class="mlogo"><svg aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path fill="#4d6bfe" d="M23.748       <div class="credits-note">
        <i class="nmark" aria-hidden="true">i</i>
        <p><strong data-i18n="credits.noteLabel">About this offer</strong><span data-i18n="credits.note">The 30,000 credits are granted automatically after installation and stay valid for one month. Offer eligibility and any regional restrictions are shown inside the extension. Free credits are optional. You can keep using your own API key with any supported provider at any time. The extension itself remains free and Lumen AI does not charge for it.</span></p>
      </div>
    </div>
  </section>

  <!-- ============ PRIVACY ============ -->
  <section class="section privacy" id="privacy">
    <div class="wrap">
      <div class="shead">
        <span class="eyebrow" data-i18n="priv.eyebrow">Privacy &amp; Security</span>
        <h2 data-i18n="priv.title">No backend. No data collection. Your key stays yours.</h2>
        <p data-i18n="priv.lede">Lumen AI has no backend server. Your privacy is built into the architecture, not added as a promise.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="pgrid">
        <div class="pcard"><span class="tick" aria-hidden="true">&#10003;</span><h3 data-i18n="priv.1t">Zero backend server</h3><p data-i18n="priv.1d">The extension has no server of its own. There is no Lumen AI service that your data could be sent to.</p></div>
        <div class="pcard"><span class="tick" aria-hidden="true">&#10003;</span><h3 data-i18n="priv.2t">No chat or browsing data collected</h3><p data-i18n="priv.2d">We do not collect your chat content, browsing history, page content or notes.</p></div>
        <div class="pcard"><span class="tick" aria-hidden="true">&#10003;</span><h3 data-i18n="priv.3t">API key stored locally</h3><p data-i18n="priv.3d">Your API key is stored only in your own browser and is never uploaded to any server of ours.</p></div>
        <div class="pcard"><span class="tick" aria-hidden="true">&#10003;</span><h3 data-i18n="priv.4t">Direct requests with your own key</h3><p data-i18n="priv.4d">When you use your own API key, AI requests are sent directly from the extension to the AI service provider you selected. Promotional free credits are a separate optional offer.</p></div>
      </div>

      <div class="flow" aria-label="Data flow">
        <p class="flow-label" data-i18n="flow.label">How a request travels</p>
        <div class="flow-row">
          <div class="flow-box"><b data-i18n="flow.1">Your browser</b><small data-i18n="flow.1s">Lumen AI sidebar extension</small></div>
          <span class="flow-arrow" aria-hidden="true">&#8594;</span>
          <div class="flow-box"><b data-i18n="flow.2">Your own API key</b><small data-i18n="flow.2s">Stored locally in the browser</small></div>
          <span class="flow-arrow" aria-hidden="true">&#8594;</span>
          <div class="flow-box"><b data-i18n="flow.3">Your chosen AI provider</b><small data-i18n="flow.3s">The service you picked</small></div>
        </div>
        <p class="flow-note" data-i18n="flow.note">&#10007; No Lumen AI server in between. &#10007; No user data uploaded to us.</p>
      </div>

      <div class="billing"><span class="mark" aria-hidden="true">&#10003;</span><p><b data-i18n="priv.billing.lead">The extension itself is completely free</b><span data-i18n="priv.billing.rest"> &mdash; no ads, no subscription fee and no registration. Beyond any promotional free trial credits, AI usage costs are charged by your own API service provider according to that provider's pricing, not by Lumen AI.</span></p></div>
    </div>
  </section>

  <!-- ============ FAQ ============ -->
  <section class="section alt faq" id="faq">
    <div class="wrap">
      <div class="shead">
        <span class="eyebrow" data-i18n="faq.eyebrow">FAQ</span>
        <h2 data-i18n="faq.title">Frequently asked questions</h2>
      </div>
      <details class="faq-item" open><summary data-i18n="faq.q1">Do I need to register an account?</summary><div class="a"><p data-i18n="faq.a1">No. There is no sign-up, no account and no login. Install the extension, enter your own AI API key in the sidebar settings, and it is ready to use. This is a no-subscription ai extension.</p></div></details>
      <details class="faq-item"><summary data-i18n="faq.q2">Will you collect my browsing history or chat data?</summary><div class="a"><p data-i18n="faq.a2">No. Lumen AI has no backend server and does not collect your chat content, browsing history, web page content or notes. Your API key is stored locally in your browser. AI requests are forwarded directly to the AI provider you chose.</p></div></details>
      <details class="faq-item"><summary data-i18n="faq.q3">What are the charges and pricing rules?</summary><div class="a"><p data-i18n="faq.a3">The extension is free, with no ads and no subscription fee. Every new install is automatically granted 30,000 credits that stay valid for one month. Beyond those credits you use your own API key, and usage fees are billed by your own AI service provider under its own pricing. Lumen AI does not charge you.</p></div></details>
      <details class="faq-item"><summary data-i18n="faq.q4">Which AI models are supported?</summary><div class="a"><p data-i18n="faq.a4">The extension connects to major AI providers through your own API key, so the models available depend on the provider and key you configure. This bring your own api key extension lets you choose the provider that fits your work.</p></div></details>
      <details class="faq-item"><summary data-i18n="faq.q5">How do I use the keyboard shortcut?</summary><div class="a"><p data-i18n="faq.a5">Open your browser's extension shortcuts page (chrome://extensions/shortcuts in Chrome, or the extensions page in Edge) and assign a shortcut to open the Lumen AI side panel. You can also open the sidebar from the extension icon or right-click menu.</p></div></details>
    </div>
  </section>

  <!-- ============ LEGAL SUMMARIES (full documents: privacy.html / terms.html) ============ -->
  <section class="policies" id="policies">
    <div class="wrap">
      <div class="shead">
        <span class="eyebrow" data-i18n="pol.eyebrow">Legal</span>
        <h2 data-i18n="pol.title">Privacy Policy &amp; Terms of Service</h2>
      </div>
      <div class="pol-grid">
        <div class="pol">
          <h3 data-i18n="pol.pt">Privacy Policy</h3>
          <p data-i18n="pol.pb">Lumen AI is a browser sidebar extension for Chrome and Edge. It has no backend server. We do not collect your chat content, browsing history, web page content or notes. Your AI API key is stored locally in your browser and is never uploaded to a server of ours. AI requests are sent directly to the AI service provider you selected. The extension shows no ads and does not use analytics or tracking. For support or privacy questions, use the Contact Us link in the footer.</p>
          <a class="pol-more legal-link" href="privacy.html" data-i18n="foot.privacy">Privacy Policy</a>
        </div>
        <div class="pol">
          <h3 data-i18n="pol.tt">Terms of Service</h3>
          <p data-i18n="pol.tb">The extension is provided free of charge, with no subscription and no account required. You are responsible for obtaining and configuring your own AI API key, and for any fees charged by your chosen AI service provider except usage covered by promotional free trial credits. Every new install is automatically granted 30,000 credits that stay valid for one month; offer eligibility and any regional restrictions are shown inside the extension. Lumen AI is a client-side tool only; it does not provide AI services itself and does not resell API access. Install the extension only from the official Chrome Web Store or Microsoft Edge Add-ons store.</p>
          <a class="pol-more legal-link" href="terms.html" data-i18n="foot.terms">Terms of Service</a>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </section>
</main>

<!-- ============ FOOTER ============ -->
<footer id="contact">
  <div class="wrap">

      "compliance.text":"這是一個瀏覽器側邊欄擴充功能。點擊按鈕將前往官方擴充功能商店進行安裝。",
      "compliance.sub":"Lumen AI 是 Chrome 與 Edge 瀏覽器側邊欄擴充功能，安裝會在官方擴充功能商店完成。本頁不會下載任何軟體或檔案。",
      "btn.chrome":"前往 Chrome 線上應用程式商店取得",
      "btn.chromeSub":"Chrome 線上應用程式商店 · 免費",
      "btn.edge":"前往 Microsoft Edge 取得",
      "btn.edgeSub":"Edge 附加元件商店 · 免費",
      "btn.recommended":"推薦用於目前瀏覽器",
      "btn.also":"也可用於其他瀏覽器",
      "hero.tagline":"無須註冊、無訂閱費用，使用你自己的 AI API 金鑰。",
      "hero.sub":"簡潔而功能完整，AI 精準翻譯。一款輕量的無廣告瀏覽器 AI 工具，深受需要閱讀、翻譯與記錄網頁筆記的使用者喜愛。",
      "hero.promo":"現在安裝自動贈送 30000 積分，一個月內有效",
      "mock.example":"範例",
      "mock.sel":"網頁中選取的文字",
      "mock.tag":"側邊欄",
      "mock.ask":"摘要這個網頁",
      "mock.reply":"重點",
      "mock.c1":"摘要",
      "mock.c2":"翻譯",
      "mock.c3":"提問",
      "mock.input":"針對目前頁面提問",
      "mock.foot":"本機 API 金鑰 · 無後端伺服器",
      "feat.eyebrow":"核心功能",
      "feat.title":"側邊欄裡的精簡工具組",
      "feat.lede":"專為閱讀、寫作與資料整理打造的 AI 側邊欄助理。所有功能都在 Chrome 與 Edge 瀏覽器側邊欄內執行，不需帳號、無廣告、無訂閱。",
      "feat.1t":"選取文字即可提問、翻譯、解析",
      "feat.1d":"在網頁上選取任意文字，即可向 AI 提問、翻譯或解析，後續追問會保留多輪對話脈絡。",
      "feat.3t":"網頁翻譯與雙語對照",
      "feat.3d":"翻譯選取詞句或整個網頁，支援外貿情境的雙語對照閱讀。",
      "feat.4t":"瀏覽時快速本機筆記",
      "feat.4d":"邊瀏覽邊記錄，筆記僅儲存在你自己的瀏覽器本機，不上傳伺服器。",
      "feat.5t":"截圖辨識與圖片文字擷取",
      "feat.5d":"上傳截圖讓 AI 辨識圖片，或擷取圖片中的文字用於閱讀與翻譯。",
      "feat.6t":"偵測並儲存網頁影音",
      "feat.6d":"偵測目前網頁中的音訊與視訊資源，依需要儲存到本機。",
      "feat.7t":"接入各大廠商 AI",
      "feat.7d":"透過你自己的 API 金鑰接入主流 AI 服務商，模型與服務商由你選擇。",
      "feat.foot":"無論你需要 Chrome AI 側邊欄擴充功能還是 Edge 側邊欄，Lumen AI 都把網頁摘要、網頁翻譯、瀏覽器內免費 AI 對話與外貿翻譯能力，整合在這一款無訂閱的擴充功能中。",
      "credits.eyebrow":"安裝即贈免費額度",
      "credits.title":"現在安裝，贈送 30000 積分",
      "credits.lede":"新安裝使用者自動獲得 30000 積分，自發放起一個月內有效，可用於下方模型。擴充功能本體依舊免費，無訂閱、無廣告。",
      "credits.listLabel":"免費額度可用模型",
      "credits.more":"等更多模型",
      "credits.noteLabel":"活動說明",
      "credits.note":"30000 積分在安裝後自動發放，自發放起一個月內有效。領取條件與適用地區以擴充功能內顯示為準。免費額度為可選福利，你隨時可以繼續使用自己的 API 金鑰接入支援的服務商。擴充功能本體始終免費，Lumen AI 不收取費用。",
      "priv.eyebrow":"隱私與安全",
      "priv.title":"零後端、不收集資料，金鑰始終屬於你",
      "priv.lede":"Lumen AI 沒有後端伺服器。隱私來自產品架構本身，而不只是一句承諾。",
      "priv.1t":"零後端伺服器",
      "priv.1d":"擴充功能沒有自有伺服器，也不存在可供上傳資料的 Lumen AI 服務。",
      "priv.2t":"不收集對話與瀏覽資料",
      "priv.2d":"我們不收集你的對話內容、瀏覽記錄、網頁內容或筆記。",
      "priv.3t":"API 金鑰僅本機儲存",
      "priv.3d":"API 金鑰只儲存在你自己的瀏覽器中，絕不會上傳到我方任何伺服器。",
      "priv.4t":"使用自己的金鑰時直連服務商",
      "priv.4d":"使用你自己的 API 金鑰時，AI 請求由擴充功能直接傳送給你所選的 AI 服務商。贈送的免費額度為獨立的可選活動。",
      "flow.label":"請求是如何傳送的",
      "flow.1":"你的瀏覽器",
      "flow.1s":"Lumen AI 側邊欄擴充功能",
      "flow.2":"你自己的 API 金鑰",
      "flow.2s":"僅儲存在瀏覽器本機",
      "flow.3":"你選擇的 AI 服務商",
      "flow.3s":"由你自行指定",
      "flow.note":"✗ 中間沒有 Lumen AI 伺服器。✗ 沒有任何使用者資料上傳給我們。",
      "priv.billing.lead":"擴充功能本體完全免費",
      "priv.billing.rest":"——無廣告、無訂閱費、無須註冊。超出活動贈送的免費額度後，AI 用量費用由你自己的 API 服務商按其定價收取，與 Lumen AI 無關。",
      "faq.eyebrow":"常見問題",
      "faq.title":"你可能想了解的問題",
      "faq.q1":"需要註冊帳號嗎？",
      "faq.a1":"不需要。沒有註冊、沒有帳號、沒有登入。安裝擴充功能後，在側邊欄設定中填入自己的 AI API 金鑰即可使用。這是一款無訂閱費用的 AI 擴充功能。",
      "faq.q2":"會收集我的瀏覽記錄或對話資料嗎？",
      "faq.a2":"不會。Lumen AI 沒有後端伺服器，不收集對話內容、瀏覽記錄、網頁內容或筆記。API 金鑰僅儲存在你的瀏覽器本機，AI 請求直接轉送給你選擇的 AI 服務商。",
      "faq.q3":"收費規則是怎樣的？",
      "faq.a3":"擴充功能免費，無廣告、無訂閱費。新安裝使用者自動獲得 30000 積分，自發放起一個月內有效。超出額度後使用你自己的 API 金鑰，用量費用由你所用服務商按其定價收取，Lumen AI 不向你收費。",
      "faq.q4":"支援哪些 AI 模型？",
      "faq.a4":"擴充功能透過你自己的 API 金鑰接入主流 AI 服務商，可用模型取決於你設定的服務商與金鑰。這款自備金鑰的擴充功能讓你可以自由選擇。",
      "faq.q5":"快速鍵怎麼使用？",
      "faq.a5":"在瀏覽器擴充功能快速鍵頁面（Chrome 為 chrome://extensions/shortcuts，Edge 在擴充功能管理頁）為 Lumen AI 側邊欄設定快速鍵即可。也可以點擊擴充功能圖示或使用右鍵選單開啟側邊欄。",
      "pol.eyebrow":"法律條款",
      "pol.title":"隱私權政策與使用條款",
      "pol.pt":"隱私權政策",
      "pol.pb":"Lumen AI 是適用於 Chrome 與 Edge 的瀏覽器側邊欄擴充功能，沒有後端伺服器。我們不收集你的對話內容、瀏覽記錄、網頁內容或筆記。AI API 金鑰僅儲存在你的瀏覽器本機，絕不會上傳到我方伺服器。AI 請求直接傳送給你所選的 AI 服務商。擴充功能無廣告，也不含任何統計或追蹤程式碼。如有支援或隱私問題，請透過頁腳的「聯絡我們」入口聯繫。",
      "pol.tt":"使用條款",
      "pol.tb":"擴充功能免費提供，無需訂閱或註冊帳號。你需要自行取得並設定 AI API 金鑰，並負擔所選服務商收取的費用，但活動贈送的免費額度所涵蓋的用量除外。新安裝使用者自動獲得 30000 積分，一個月內有效；領取條件與適用地區以擴充功能內顯示為準。Lumen AI 僅是用戶端工具，不自行提供 AI 服務，也不轉售 API 存取權。請僅從官方 Chrome 線上應用程式商店或 Microsoft Edge 附加元件商店安裝。",
      "foot.about":"適用於 Chrome 與 Edge 的免費、無廣告 AI 側邊欄助理。網頁摘要、外貿翻譯、瀏覽器內 AI 對話與本機筆記，使用你自己的 API 金鑰，無訂閱費用。",
      "foot.product":"產品",
      "foot.privacyLink":"隱私與安全",
      "foot.legal":"法律條款",
      "foot.connect":"聯絡與分享",
      "foot.privacy":"隱私權政策",
      "foot.terms":"使用條款",
      "foot.share":"分享本頁",
      "foot.rights":"保留所有權利。",
      "foot.tagline":"Lumen AI 是適用於 Chrome 與 Edge 的瀏覽器側邊欄擴充功能。",
      "credits.provider":"積分由以下平台發放：",
      "credits.providerNote":"該平台自身的條款與隱私權政策適用於積分。",
      "foot.operator":"營運主體：個人 shijian zhang。"
    },

      "credits.fact1":"Otorgado automáticamente al instalar",

      "skip":"Ir al contenido",
      "brand.sub":"Asistente de IA en la barra lateral",

      "share.copy":"Copiar enlace",
      "share.copied":"Enlace copiado",
      "share.email":"Compartir por correo",
      "hero.title":"Tu asistente de IA en cada página web",
      "hero.lede":"Lumen AI es un asistente de IA gratuito en la barra lateral para Chrome y Edge. Resume páginas web, traduce sitios para comercio exterior y chatea gratis con IA mientras lees, sin salir de la página en la que estás.",
      "chip.free":"Asistente de IA gratuito",
      "chip.noads":"Sin anuncios",
      "chip.byok":"Usa tu propia clave API",
      "chip.nosub":"Sin suscripción",
      "compliance.label":"Lee esto antes de instalar",
      "compliance.text":"Esta es una extensión de barra lateral del navegador. Al pulsar el botón accederás a la tienda oficial de extensiones para instalarla.",
      "compliance.sub":"Lumen AI es una extensión de barra lateral para Chrome y Edge. La instalación se realiza en la tienda oficial de extensiones. Desde esta página no se descarga ningún software ni archivo.",
      "btn.chrome":"Obtener en Chrome Web Store",
      "btn.chromeSub":"Chrome Web Store · Gratis",
      "btn.edge":"Obtener para Microsoft Edge",
      "btn.edgeSub":"Tienda de complementos de Edge · Gratis",
      "btn.recommended":"Recomendado para tu navegador",
      "btn.also":"También disponible",
      "hero.tagline":"Sin registro, sin suscripción. Usa tu propia clave de API de IA.",
      "hero.sub":"Funciones simples y potentes. Traducción precisa con IA. Una herramienta de IA ligera y sin anuncios, popular entre quienes leen, traducen y toman notas en la web.",
      "hero.promo":"¿Instalando ahora? Obtenga 30,000 créditos gratis, válidos por un mes",
      "mock.example":"Ejemplo",
      "mock.sel":"Texto seleccionado en la página",
      "mock.tag":"Barra lateral",
      "mock.ask":"Resume esta página",
      "mock.reply":"Puntos clave",
      "mock.c1":"Resumir",
      "mock.c2":"Traducir",
      "mock.c3":"Preguntar",
      "mock.input":"Pregunta lo que quieras sobre esta página",
      "mock.foot":"Clave API local · Sin servidor propio",
      "feat.eyebrow":"Funciónes",
      "feat.title":"Un conjunto de herramientas en tu barra lateral",
      "feat.lede":"Un asistente de IA para leer, escribir e investigar. Todas las funciones se ejecutan en la barra lateral de Chrome y Edge: sin cuenta, sin anuncios y sin suscripción.",
      "feat.1t":"Pregunta sobre el texto seleccionado",
      "feat.1d":"Selecciona cualquier texto de una página y pregunta a la IA. Las preguntas posteriores conservan el contexto de la conversación.",
      "feat.3t":"Traducción de páginas y vista bilingüe",
      "feat.3d":"Traduce una frase seleccionada o la página completa. Lectura bilingüe en paralelo para trabajos de traducción en comercio exterior.",
      "feat.4t":"Notas locales rápidas",
      "feat.4d":"Toma notas mientras navegas. Se guardan localmente en tu propio navegador, no en un servidor.",
      "feat.5t":"Capturas y texto en imágenes",
      "feat.5d":"Sube una captura para preguntar a la IA sobre la imagen o extrae su texto para leer y traducir.",
      "feat.6t":"Detectar y guardar multimedia",
      "feat.6d":"Detecta recursos de audio y vídeo en la página actual y guárdalos localmente cuando los necesites.",
      "feat.7t":"Conecta tu proveedor de IA",
      "feat.7d":"Funciona con los principales proveedores de IA mediante tu propia clave API. Tú eliges el modelo y el proveedor.",
      "feat.foot":"Tanto si necesitas una extensión de barra lateral con IA para Chrome como para Edge, Lumen AI reúne resumen de páginas, traducción web, chat de IA gratuito y traducción para comercio exterior en una extensión sin suscripción.",
      "credits.eyebrow":"Créditos gratis con cada instalación",
      "credits.title":"Instálelo ahora y obtenga 30,000 créditos gratis",
      "credits.lede":"Cada nueva instalación recibe automáticamente créditos 30,000, válidos por un mes. Úsalos con los modelos siguientes. La extensión en sí sigue siendo gratuita, sin suscripción ni anuncios.",
      "credits.listLabel":"Modelos disponibles con los créditos gratuitos",
      "credits.more":"y más modelos",
      "credits.noteLabel":"Sobre esta oferta",
      "credits.note":"Los créditos 30,000 se otorgan automáticamente después de la instalación y son válidos durante un mes. La elegibilidad de la oferta y las restricciones regionales se muestran dentro de la extensión. Los créditos gratuitos son opcionales. Puede seguir usando su propia clave API con cualquier proveedor compatible en cualquier momento. La extensión en sí sigue siendo gratuita y Lumen AI no cobra por ella.",
      "priv.eyebrow":"Privacidad y seguridad",
      "priv.title":"Sin backend. Sin recopilación de datos. Tu clave sigue siendo tuya.",
      "priv.lede":"Lumen AI no tiene servidor backend. Tu privacidad forma parte de la arquitectura, no es solo una promesa.",
      "priv.1t":"Cero servidores backend",
      "priv.1d":"La extensión no tiene servidor propio. No existe ningún servicio de Lumen AI al que pudieran enviarse tus datos.",
      "priv.2t":"No recopilamos chats ni navegación",
      "priv.2d":"No recopilamos el contenido de tus chats, tu historial de navegación, el contenido de las páginas ni tus notas.",
      "priv.3t":"Clave API almacenada localmente",
      "priv.3d":"Tu clave API se guarda únicamente en tu propio navegador y nunca se sube a ningún servidor nuestro.",
      "priv.4t":"Peticiones directas con tu propia clave",
      "priv.4d":"Cuando usas tu propia clave API, las peticiones se envían directamente desde la extensión al proveedor de IA que elegiste. Los créditos promocionales son una oferta independiente y opcional.",
      "flow.label":"Cómo viaja una petición",
      "flow.1":"Tu navegador",
      "flow.1s":"Extensión de barra lateral Lumen AI",
      "flow.2":"Tu propia clave API",
      "flow.2s":"Almacenada localmente en el navegador",
      "flow.3":"Tu proveedor de IA elegido",
      "flow.3s":"El servicio que seleccionaste",
      "flow.note":"✗ Ningún servidor de Lumen AI intermedio. ✗ Ningún dato de usuario subido a nosotros.",
      "priv.billing.lead":"La extensión es completamente gratuita",
      "priv.billing.rest":" — sin anuncios, sin suscripción y sin registro. Más allá de los créditos promocionales gratuitos, el uso de IA lo factura tu propio proveedor de API según sus tarifas, no Lumen AI.",
      "faq.eyebrow":"Preguntas frecuentes",
      "faq.title":"Preguntas habituales",
      "faq.q1":"¿Necesito registrar una cuenta?",
      "faq.a1":"No. No hay registro, cuenta ni inicio de sesión. Instala la extensión, introduce tu propia clave de API de IA en los ajustes de la barra lateral y ya está lista. Es una extensión de IA sin suscripción.",
      "faq.q2":"¿Recopiláis mi historial de navegación o mis chats?",
      "faq.a2":"No. Lumen AI no tiene servidor backend y no recopila el contenido de tus chats, tu historial, el contenido de las páginas ni tus notas. Tu clave API se guarda localmente en tu navegador. Las peticiones se envían directamente al proveedor de IA que elegiste.",
      "faq.q3":"¿Cuáles son los costes y las tarifas?",
      "faq.a3":"La extensión es gratuita, sin anuncios y sin tarifa de suscripción. Cada nueva instalación recibe automáticamente 30,000 créditos que permanecen válidos durante un mes. Más allá de esos créditos, usted utiliza su propia clave API y las tarifas de uso las factura su propio proveedor de servicios de IA según sus propios precios. Lumen AI no te cobra.",
      "faq.q4":"¿Qué modelos de IA son compatibles?",
      "faq.a4":"La extensión se conecta a los principales proveedores de IA mediante tu propia clave API, así que los modelos disponibles dependen del proveedor y la clave que configures. Esta extensión con clave propia te permite elegir el proveedor que mejor se adapte a tu trabajo.",
      "faq.q5":"¿Cómo uso el atajo de teclado?",
      "faq.a5":"Abre la página de atajos de extensiones de tu navegador (chrome://extensions/shortcuts en Chrome, o la página de extensiones en Edge) y asigna un atajo para abrir el panel lateral de Lumen AI. También puedes abrir la barra lateral desde el icono de la extensión o el menú contextual.",
      "pol.eyebrow":"Legal",
      "pol.title":"Política de privacidad y condiciones del servicio",
      "pol.pt":"Política de privacidad",
      "pol.pb":"Lumen AI es una extensión de barra lateral para Chrome y Edge. No tiene servidor backend. No recopilamos el contenido de tus chats, tu historial de navegación, el contenido de las páginas ni tus notas. Tu clave de API de IA se guarda localmente en tu navegador y nunca se sube a un servidor nuestro. Las peticiones de IA se envían directamente al proveedor que elegiste. La extensión no muestra anuncios y no utiliza analítica ni seguimiento. Para consultas de soporte o privacidad, usa el enlace de contacto del pie de página.",
      "pol.tt":"Condiciones del servicio",
      "pol.tb":"La extensión se proporciona de forma gratuita, sin suscripción ni necesidad de cuenta. Usted es responsable de obtener y configurar su propia clave API de IA y de cualquier tarifa cobrada por el proveedor de servicios de IA elegido, excepto el uso cubierto por créditos de prueba gratuitos promocionales. Cada nueva instalación recibe automáticamente 30,000 créditos que permanecen válidos durante un mes; La elegibilidad de la oferta y las restricciones regionales se muestran dentro de la extensión. Lumen AI es únicamente una herramienta del lado del cliente; no proporciona servicios de IA por sí mismo y no revende acceso a API. Instale la extensión únicamente desde la tienda oficial Chrome Web Store o Microsoft Edge Add-ons store.",
      "foot.about":"Un asistente de IA gratuito y sin anuncios para Chrome y Edge. Resume páginas, traduce para comercio exterior, chatea con IA y guarda notas locales, con tu propia clave API y sin suscripción.",
      "foot.product":"Producto",
      "foot.privacyLink":"Privacidad y seguridad",
      "foot.legal":"Legal",
      "foot.connect":"Contacto y compartir",
      "foot.privacy":"Política de privacidad",
      "foot.terms":"Condiciones del servicio",
      "foot.share":"Compartir esta página",
      "foot.rights":"Todos los derechos reservados.",
      "foot.tagline":"Lumen AI es una extensión de barra lateral para Chrome y Edge.",
      "credits.provider":"Proporcionado por esta plataforma independiente:",
      "credits.providerNote":"Se aplican los términos y la política de privacidad propios de esa plataforma.",
      "foot.operator":"Operado por shijian zhang (un individuo)."
    },
